function U(name,mark,speed,attack,shape,jutsuShape,jutsuCost,jutsuDamage,linkBuff=null,maxHp=110,maxChakra=8,defense=0,archetype='unassigned'){
 return{name,mark,speed,attack,defense,archetype,shape,jutsuShape,jutsuCost,jutsuDamage,linkBuff,hp:maxHp,maxHp,chakra:2,maxChakra,rotation:0}

// v0.6.7 DATA AUTHORITY
// Canonical stats/roles/shapes/ability costs live in each unit's data/unit.json.
// The standalone embeds a generated copy in window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA so mobile local-file play needs no fetch().
const UNIT_DATA=window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA||{};
const UNIT_NAME_TO_ID=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(UNIT_DATA).map(([id,d])=>[d.display_name,id])));
function canonicalUnit(nameOrId){
 const id=UNIT_DATA[nameOrId]?nameOrId:UNIT_NAME_TO_ID[nameOrId];
 const d=id?UNIT_DATA[id]:null;
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown canonical unit: ${nameOrId}`);
 return d;
}
function runtimeDefinition(nameOrId){
 const d=canonicalUnit(nameOrId),basic=d.abilities.basic||{},jutsu=d.abilities.jutsu||{};
 return{
  mark:d.combat.mark,speed:d.stats.speed,attack:d.stats.attack,defense:d.stats.defense,
  hp:d.stats.hp,maxHp:d.stats.hp,shape:d.combat.basic_shape,jutsuShape:d.combat.jutsu_shape||{type:'circle',r:0},
  maxChakra:d.combat.chakra_max||0,jutsuCost:jutsu.cost??99,
  jutsuDamage:jutsu?Math.round(d.stats.attack*(jutsu.damage_multiplier||1)):0,
  jutsuName:jutsu?.name||'Jutsu',archetype:d.archetype,render:d.render||{},
  linkBuff:d.combat.link_buff||null
 };
}
const BATTLE_ROSTER=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(
 ['crimson','subzero','lebee','senku','anubis']
   .filter(id=>UNIT_DATA[id])
   .map(id=>[UNIT_DATA[id].display_name,runtimeDefinition(id)])
));
function makeRosterUnit(name,opts={}){
 const d=BATTLE_ROSTER[name];
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown battle unit: ${name}`);
 const u=U(name,d.mark,d.speed,d.attack,d.shape,d.jutsuShape,d.jutsuCost,d.jutsuDamage,d.linkBuff||null,d.maxHp,d.maxChakra,d.defense,d.archetype);
 u.jutsuName=d.jutsuName||'Jutsu';
 u.renderScale=d.render?.scale||1;
 const start=opts.startingChakra??2;
 u.chakra=start==='full'?u.maxChakra:Math.max(0,Math.min(u.maxChakra,start));
 return u;
}
function makeEnemyFromData(id,name,x,y,mark,overrides={}){
 const d=canonicalUnit(id),base=d.stats,combat=d.combat;
 const hp=overrides.hp??base.hp;
 return{name:name||d.display_name,spriteKey:d.display_name,mark:mark||combat.mark,x,y,r:overrides.r??19,
  hp,maxHp:hp,speed:overrides.speed??base.speed,attack:overrides.attack??base.attack,defense:overrides.defense??base.defense,
  gauge:0,shape:overrides.shape??combat.basic_shape,archetype:d.archetype};
}

   console.error('Playable roster registration missing:',missing);
   return false;
 }
 return true;
}

// v0.6.7 DATA AUTHORITY
// Canonical stats/roles/shapes/ability costs live in each unit's data/unit.json.
// The standalone embeds a generated copy in window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA so mobile local-file play needs no fetch().
const UNIT_DATA=window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA||{};
const UNIT_NAME_TO_ID=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(UNIT_DATA).map(([id,d])=>[d.display_name,id])));
function canonicalUnit(nameOrId){
 const id=UNIT_DATA[nameOrId]?nameOrId:UNIT_NAME_TO_ID[nameOrId];
 const d=id?UNIT_DATA[id]:null;
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown canonical unit: ${nameOrId}`);
 return d;
}
function runtimeDefinition(nameOrId){
 const d=canonicalUnit(nameOrId),basic=d.abilities.basic||{},jutsu=d.abilities.jutsu||{};
 return{
  mark:d.combat.mark,speed:d.stats.speed,attack:d.stats.attack,defense:d.stats.defense,
  hp:d.stats.hp,maxHp:d.stats.hp,shape:d.combat.basic_shape,jutsuShape:d.combat.jutsu_shape||{type:'circle',r:0},
  maxChakra:d.combat.chakra_max||0,jutsuCost:jutsu.cost??99,
  jutsuDamage:jutsu?Math.round(d.stats.attack*(jutsu.damage_multiplier||1)):0,
  jutsuName:jutsu?.name||'Jutsu',archetype:d.archetype,render:d.render||{},
  linkBuff:d.combat.link_buff||null
 };
}
const BATTLE_ROSTER=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(
 ['crimson','subzero','lebee','senku','anubis']
   .filter(id=>UNIT_DATA[id])
   .map(id=>[UNIT_DATA[id].display_name,runtimeDefinition(id)])
));
function makeRosterUnit(name,opts={}){
 const d=BATTLE_ROSTER[name];
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown battle unit: ${name}`);
 const u=U(name,d.mark,d.speed,d.attack,d.shape,d.jutsuShape,d.jutsuCost,d.jutsuDamage,d.linkBuff||null,d.maxHp,d.maxChakra,d.defense,d.archetype);
 u.jutsuName=d.jutsuName||'Jutsu';
 u.renderScale=d.render?.scale||1;
 const start=opts.startingChakra??2;
 u.chakra=start==='full'?u.maxChakra:Math.max(0,Math.min(u.maxChakra,start));
 return u;
}
function makeEnemyFromData(id,name,x,y,mark,overrides={}){
 const d=canonicalUnit(id),base=d.stats,combat=d.combat;
 const hp=overrides.hp??base.hp;
 return{name:name||d.display_name,spriteKey:d.display_name,mark:mark||combat.mark,x,y,r:overrides.r??19,
  hp,maxHp:hp,speed:overrides.speed??base.speed,attack:overrides.attack??base.attack,defense:overrides.defense??base.defense,
  gauge:0,shape:overrides.shape??combat.basic_shape,archetype:d.archetype};
}

function buildPlayerPairs(y){
 const xs=[95,240,385];
 const team=getActiveTeam();

 return team.map((name,i)=>{
   if(!BATTLE_ROSTER[name]){
     console.error(`Team unit "${name}" is not registered in BATTLE_ROSTER.`);
     const fallback=DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM.find(n=>BATTLE_ROSTER[n]&&!team.includes(n))
       || DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM.find(n=>BATTLE_ROSTER[n])
       || Object.keys(BATTLE_ROSTER)[0];
     name=fallback;
   }

   return {
     x:xs[i],y,active:0,gauge:0,units:[
       makeRosterUnit(name,teamSpawnOptions(name)),
       emptyReserve()
     ]
   };
 });
}

// Ability-tier lookup must live at battle scope because both the ground-field
// pass and the later enemy target-feedback pass use it.
function isUltimateAction(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   // canonicalUnit already resolves both IDs and display names through UNIT_NAME_TO_ID.
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return !!(ability?.ultimate===true || ability?.category==='ultimate' || ability?.tier==='ultimate');
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}

function freshBoss(){
 return {
  time:0,phase:'charge',action:'normal',ready:null,readyStarted:0,_chargeSince:performance.now(),drag:false,dragOrigin:null,dragVisual:null,anim:null,floaters:[],victoryFX:null,jutsuDim:null,
  log:'BOSS BATTLE — Anubis, Judge of Souls.',
  bossBattle:true,battleMode:'boss',
  pairs:buildPlayerPairs(585),
  enemies:[
   {
    name:'Anubis',spriteKey:'Anubis',mark:canonicalUnit('anubis').combat.mark,
    x:240,y:195,r:29,
    hp:canonicalUnit('anubis').stats.hp,maxHp:canonicalUnit('anubis').stats.hp,
    speed:canonicalUnit('anubis').stats.speed,attack:canonicalUnit('anubis').stats.attack,defense:canonicalUnit('anubis').stats.defense,gauge:0,
    shape:canonicalUnit('anubis').combat.basic_shape,
    boss:true,sizeScale:canonicalUnit('anubis').render.battle_size_scale||2.0625
   }
  ]
 };
}

let S=fresh();

const front=p=>p.units[p.active], back=p=>p.units[1-p.active];
const pairAlive=p=>p.units.some(u=>u.hp>0);
const alivePairs=()=>S.pairs.filter(pairAlive);
const aliveEnemies=()=>S.enemies.filter(e=>e.hp>0);
const NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE={type:'circle',r:43};
const normalShape=()=>NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE;
const attackShape=u=>{
 // Strict separation:
 // NORMAL -> unit's canonical combat.basic_shape
 // JUTSU  -> unit's canonical combat.jutsu_shape
 if(S.action==='jutsu')return u.jutsuShape;
 return u.shape||normalShape();
};
function attackProxy(u){return {...u,shape:attackShape(u)}}

// Ability-tier lookup must live at battle scope because both the ground-field
// pass and the later enemy target-feedback pass use it.
function isUltimateAction(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   // canonicalUnit already resolves both IDs and display names through UNIT_NAME_TO_ID.
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return !!(ability?.ultimate===true || ability?.category==='ultimate' || ability?.tier==='ultimate');
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}

function isAllySupportJutsu(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return ability?.target==='all_allies' || ability?.effect==='heal_full';
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}


function ensureFront(p){
 if(front(p).hp>0)return true;
 if(front(p).hp<=0 && koAnimating(front(p).name))return true;
 if(back(p).hp>0){p.active=1-p.active;return true}
 return false
}
function fxMap(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.unitFX)S.anim.unitFX={};
 return S.anim.unitFX;
}
function unitFX(name){
 return S.anim?.unitFX?.[name]||null;
}
function koAnimating(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration);
}
function renderWhileKO(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration+90);
}
function startHitReaction(name,dir=1,strong=false){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'hit',start:performance.now(),duration:strong?230:180,dir:dir||1,strong:!!strong};
}
function startKOReaction(name,dir=1){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'ko',start:performance.now(),duration:520,dir:dir||1};
}
function pairRenderable(p){
 return pairAlive(p)||renderWhileKO(front(p).name)||renderWhileKO(back(p).name);
}

 addFloat(p.x,p.y-58,'KO','#ff7272');

       S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${front(p).name} automatically moved to the front.`;
     }
   },380);
 }else{
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — that pair has been def

function drawActiveLinkSummary(pair,pos,spriteTop){
 if(S.phase!=='player'||S.ready?.kind!=='pair'||S.ready.ref!==pair)
   return;

 const previewing=(S.drag||S.action==='jutsu');
 if(!previewing)return;

 const members=comboMembers(null,pair);
 if(members.length<=1)return;

 const u=front(pair);
 let damage=0,baseDamage=0;

 if(S.action==='jutsu'){
   const helpers=members.filter(p=>p!==pair);
   damage=u.jutsuDamage+helpers.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+buffedNormalDamage(p).damage,0);
   baseDamage=u.jutsuDamage+helpers.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+front(p).attack,0);
 }else{
   damage=members.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+buffedNormalDamage(p).damage,0);
   baseDamage=members.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+front(p).attack,0);
 }

 const bonus=baseDamage>0?Math.max(0,damage/baseDamage-1):0;
 const strength=linkVisualStrength(pair);
 const sat=Math.round(28+72*strength);
 const alpha=.45+.50*strength;
 const accent=`hsla(143,${sat}%,58%,${alpha})`;

 const label=`LINK ×${members.length} · ${damage} DMG${bonus>0?` · +${Math.round(bonus*100)}%`:''}`;
 const y=spriteTop-27;

 ctx.save();
 ctx.font='bold 9px system-ui';
 ctx.textAlign='center';
 const width=Math.max(84,ctx.measureText(label).width+12);

 ctx.fillStyle='rgba(7,10,12,.68)';
 ctx.fillRect(pos.x-width/2,y-12,width,17);

 ctx.strokeStyle=accent;
 ctx.lineWidth=.8;
 ctx.strokeRect(pos.x-width/2+.5,y-11.5,width-1,16);

 ctx.fillStyle=accent;
 ctx.fillText(label,pos.x,y);
 ctx.restore();
}

function drawCompactHP(p,u){
 let x=p.x,y=p.y+27,w=34,h=3;
 ctx.save();

 ctx.fillStyle='rgba(5,9,11,.55)';
 ctx.fillRect(x-w/2,y,w,h);

 let ratio=Math.max(0,u.hp/u.maxHp);
 ctx.fillStyle=ratio>.5?'#53dc72':ratio>.25?'#ffbd4a':'#ff616a';
 ctx.fillRect(x-w/2,y,w*ratio,h);

 ctx.restore()
}

function drawPlayerResources(p,u,linkState={linked:false,count:0},active=false,pairRef=null){
 const strength=pairRef?linkVisualStrength(pairRef):0;

 // Keep battlefield HUD minimal: no unit nameplates or numeric text.
 // Linked-state feedback remains above the fighter.
 if(linkState.linked){
   const spriteTop=pairRef?playerSpriteTopY(pairRef,active):p.y-56;
   drawOverheadLinkIcon(p.x,spriteTop-10,strength);
 }

 const x=p.x;
 const hpY=p.y+19;
 const hpW=52;
 const hpH=4;
 const hpRatio=clamp(u.hp/u.maxHp,0,1);

 ctx.save();

 // Health bar sits immediately beneath the feet/shadow.
 ctx.fillStyle='rgba(4,9,13,.72)';
 ctx.fillRect(x-hpW/2,hpY,hpW,hpH);
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.34)';
 ctx.lineWidth=.55;
 ctx.strokeRect(x-hpW/2+.25,hpY+.25,hpW-.5,hpH-.5);
 ctx.fillStyle=hpRatio>.5?'#53dc72':hpRatio>.25?'#ffbd4a':'#ff616a';
 ctx.fillRect(x-hpW/2,hpY,hpW*hpRatio,hpH);

 // Neon-blue chakra pips sit on their own row below HP.
 const pipW=5,pipH=4,gap=2,total=u.maxChakra;
 const chakraY=hpY+8;
 const start=x-((pipW+gap)*total-gap)/2;
 for(let i=0;i<total;i++){
   if(i<u.chakra){
     ctx.fillStyle='#23c9ff';
     ctx.shadowColor='rgba(35,201,255,.88)';
     ctx.shadowBlur=3.5;
   }else{
     ctx.fillStyle='rgba(12,25,33,.72)';
     ctx.shadowBlur=0;
   }
   ctx.fillRect(start+i*(pipW+gap),chakraY,pipW,pipH);
 }

 ctx.restore();
}

function drawMoveReturnCue(){
 if(!S.drag||!S.dragOrigin||S.ready?.kind!=='pair')return;

 const p=S.ready.ref;
 const dist=d(p,S.dragOrigin);
 const hint=dist<=MOVE_RETURN_HINT_RADIUS;
 const cancelReady=dist<=MOVE_CANCEL_RADIUS;
 const proximity=1-clamp(dist/MOVE_RETURN_HINT_RADIUS,0,1);

 ctx.save();
 ctx.translate(S.dragOrigin.x,S.dragOrigin.y+18);

 // The home point is always faintly visible while dragging.
 // It progressively brightens as the held piece approaches it.
 const alpha=.045 + proximity*.11 + (cancelReady?.10:0);
 ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,255,255,${alpha})`;
 ctx.shadowColor='rgba(255,255,255,.78)';
 ctx.shadowBlur=cancelReady?8:hint?3:1;

 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.ellipse(
   0,0,
   cancelReady?10.5:hint?9:7.5,
   cancelReady?4.1:hint?3.4:2.7,
   0,0,Math.PI*2
 );
 ctx.fill();

 // The cancel symbol appears ONLY inside the true snap/cancel core.
 if(cancelReady){
   ctx.save();
   ctx.translate(0,-10);
   ctx.rotate(-.55);

   ctx.shadowBlur=0;
   ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.94)';
   ctx.lineWidth=1.45;
   ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.arc(0,0,4.8,.35,5.55);
   ctx.stroke();

   ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.moveTo(3.9,-3.9);
   ctx.lineTo(7.5,-4.3);
   ctx.lineTo(5.8,-.9);
   ctx.closePath();
   ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.96)';
   ctx.fill();

   ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.arc(0,0,1.05,0,Math.PI*2);
   ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.90)';
   ctx.fill();

   ctx.restore();
 }

 ctx.restore();
}

 S.phase='resolve';

       SENKU_BOMB_FRAMES.length-1,
       Math.floor(t*SENKU_BOMB_FRAMES.length)
     );

     if(img?.complete&&img.naturalWidth>0){

       ctx.translate(x,y);
       ctx.rotate(angle);
       ctx.shadowColor='#ff9a32';
       ctx.shadowBlur=8;
       ctx.drawImage(img,-w/2,-h/2,w,h);
     }

 // debuffs can append their own segments later without adding map clutter.
 return items;

}

setInterval(tick,30);
function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}
    let push=minDist-len;
    p.x=clamp(p.x+(dx/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right);
    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }
}
function inputPoint(ev){
 let r=cvs.getBoundingClientRect();
 return {x:(ev.clientX-r.left)*W/r.width,y:(ev.clientY-r.top)*H/r.height};
}

// ===== v0.5.15 MOBILE INPUT =====
// Battlefield interaction is touch-first: press the active unit, drag to position,
// and release to commit the selected Normal/Jutsu action.
cvs.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{
 if(S.phase!=='player'||S.anim?.settle)return;
 let p=S.ready?.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev);
 if(!p||!pairAlive(p)||d(pt,p)>UNIT_TOUCH_RADIUS)return;
 ev.preventDefault();
 S.drag=true;
 S.dragOrigin={x:p.x,y:p.y};
 S.dragVisual={scale:1.0,rot:0,lift:0,lastX:pt.x,lastY:pt.y,targetScale:1.34,targetLift:17};
 try{cvs.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId)}catch(_e){}
});

 if(!S.drag)return;
 ev.preventDefault();
 let p=S.ready.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev),v

function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}
    let push=minDist-len;
    p.x=clamp(p.x+(dx/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right);
    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }
}

function U(name,mark,speed,attack,shape,jutsuShape,jutsuCost,jutsuDamage,linkBuff=null,maxHp=110,maxChakra=8,defense=0,archetype='unassigned'){
 return{name,mark,speed,attack,defense,archetype,shape,jutsuShape,jutsuCost,jutsuDamage,linkBuff,hp:maxHp,maxHp,chakra:2,maxChakra,rotation:0}
}

// v0.6.7 DATA AUTHORITY
// Canonical stats/roles/shapes/ability costs live in each unit's data/unit.json.
// The standalone embeds a generated copy in window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA so mobile local-file play needs no fetch().
const UNIT_DATA=window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA||{};
const UNIT_NAME_TO_ID=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(UNIT_DATA).map(([id,d])=>[d.display_name,id])));
function canonicalUnit(nameOrId){
 const id=UNIT_DATA[nameOrId]?nameOrId:UNIT_NAME_TO_ID[nameOrId];
 const d=id?UNIT_DATA[id]:null;
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown canonical unit: ${nameOrId}`);
 return d;
}
function runtimeDefinition(nameOrId){
 const d=canonicalUnit(nameOrId),basic=d.abilities.basic||{},jutsu=d.abilities.jutsu||{};
 return{
  mark:d.combat.mark,speed:d.stats.speed,attack:d.stats.attack,defense:d.stats.defense,
  hp:d.stats.hp,maxHp:d.stats.hp,shape:d.combat.basic_shape,jutsuShape:d.combat.jutsu_shape||{type:'circle',r:0},
  maxChakra:d.combat.chakra_max||0,jutsuCost:jutsu.cost??99,
  jutsuDamage:jutsu?Math.round(d.stats.attack*(jutsu.damage_multiplier||1)):0,
  jutsuName:jutsu?.name||'Jutsu',archetype:d.archetype,render:d.render||{},
  linkBuff:d.combat.link_buff||null
 };
}
const BATTLE_ROSTER=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(
 ['crimson','subzero','lebee','senku','anubis']
   .filter(id=>UNIT_DATA[id])
   .map(id=>[UNIT_DATA[id].display_name,runtimeDefinition(id)])
));
function makeRosterUnit(name,opts={}){
 const d=BATTLE_ROSTER[name];
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown battle unit: ${name}`);
 const u=U(name,d.mark,d.speed,d.attack,d.shape,d.jutsuShape,d.jutsuCost,d.jutsuDamage,d.linkBuff||null,d.maxHp,d.maxChakra,d.defense,d.archetype);
 u.jutsuName=d.jutsuName||'Jutsu';
 u.renderScale=d.render?.scale||1;
 const start=opts.startingChakra??2;
 u.chakra=start==='full'?u.maxChakra:Math.max(0,Math.min(u.maxChakra,start));
 return u;
}
function makeEnemyFromData(id,name,x,y,mark,overrides={}){
 const d=canonicalUnit(id),base=d.stats,combat=d.combat;
 const hp=overrides.hp??base.hp;
 return{name:name||d.display_name,spriteKey:d.display_name,mark:mark||combat.mark,x,y,r:overrides.r??19,
  hp,maxHp:hp,speed:overrides.speed??base.speed,attack:overrides.attack??base.attack,defense:overrides.defense??base.defense,
  gauge:0,shape:overrides.shape??combat.basic_shape,archetype:d.archetype};
}

// v0.5.19 core-roster whitelist. Legacy units stay in BATTLE_ROSTER for rollback only.
const ACTIVE_PLAYABLE_UNITS=Object.freeze(['Crimson','Sub-Zero','Lebee','Senku']);
const DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM=Object.freeze(['Crimson','Lebee','Sub-Zero']);

function validatePlayableRosterRegistration(){
 const missing=ACTIVE_PLAYABLE_UNITS.filter(name=>!BATTLE_ROSTER[name]);
 if(missing.length){
   console.error('Playable roster registration missing:',missing);
   return false;
 }
 return true;
}
const PLAYABLE_ROSTER_VALID=validatePlayableRosterRegistration();
const TEAM_STORAGE_KEY='blazingBattle.activeTeam.v1';

function validActiveTeam(team){
 return Array.isArray(team)
   && team.length===3
   && new Set(team).size===3
   && team.every(name=>ACTIVE_PLAYABLE_UNITS.includes(name));
}

 if(validActiveTeam(window.BLAZING_ACTIV

function U(name,mark,speed,attack,shape,jutsuShape,jutsuCost,jutsuDamage,linkBuff=null,maxHp=110,maxChakra=8,defense=0,archetype='unassigned'){
 return{name,mark,speed,attack,defense,archetype,shape,jutsuShape,jutsuCost,jutsuDamage,linkBuff,hp:maxHp,maxHp,chakra:2,maxChakra,rotation:0}
}

// v0.6.7 DATA AUTHORITY
// Canonical stats/roles/shapes/ability costs live in each unit's data/unit.json.
// The standalone embeds a generated copy in window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA so mobile local-file play needs no fetch().
const UNIT_DATA=window.BLAZING_UNIT_DATA||{};
const UNIT_NAME_TO_ID=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(UNIT_DATA).map(([id,d])=>[d.display_name,id])));
function canonicalUnit(nameOrId){
 const id=UNIT_DATA[nameOrId]?nameOrId:UNIT_NAME_TO_ID[nameOrId];
 const d=id?UNIT_DATA[id]:null;
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown canonical unit: ${nameOrId}`);
 return d;
}
function runtimeDefinition(nameOrId){
 const d=canonicalUnit(nameOrId),basic=d.abilities.basic||{},jutsu=d.abilities.jutsu||{};
 return{
  mark:d.combat.mark,speed:d.stats.speed,attack:d.stats.attack,defense:d.stats.defense,
  hp:d.stats.hp,maxHp:d.stats.hp,shape:d.combat.basic_shape,jutsuShape:d.combat.jutsu_shape||{type:'circle',r:0},
  maxChakra:d.combat.chakra_max||0,jutsuCost:jutsu.cost??99,
  jutsuDamage:jutsu?Math.round(d.stats.attack*(jutsu.damage_multiplier||1)):0,
  jutsuName:jutsu?.name||'Jutsu',archetype:d.archetype,render:d.render||{},
  linkBuff:d.combat.link_buff||null
 };
}
const BATTLE_ROSTER=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(
 ['crimson','subzero','lebee','senku','anubis']
   .filter(id=>UNIT_DATA[id])
   .map(id=>[UNIT_DATA[id].display_name,runtimeDefinition(id)])
));
function makeRosterUnit(name,opts={}){
 const d=BATTLE_ROSTER[name];
 if(!d)throw new Error(`Unknown battle unit: ${name}`);
 const u=U(name,d.mark,d.speed,d.attack,d.shape,d.jutsuShape,d.jutsuCost,d.jutsuDamage,d.linkBuff||null,d.maxHp,d.maxChakra,d.defense,d.archetype);
 u.jutsuName=d.jutsuName||'Jutsu';
 u.renderScale=d.render?.scale||1;
 const start=opts.startingChakra??2;
 u.chakra=start==='full'?u.maxChakra:Math.max(0,Math.min(u.maxChakra,start));
 return u;
}
function makeEnemyFromData(id,name,x,y,mark,overrides={}){
 const d=canonicalUnit(id),base=d.stats,combat=d.combat;
 const hp=overrides.hp??base.hp;
 return{name:name||d.display_name,spriteKey:d.display_name,mark:mark||combat.mark,x,y,r:overrides.r??19,
  hp,maxHp:hp,speed:overrides.speed??base.speed,attack:overrides.attack??base.attack,defense:overrides.defense??base.defense,
  gauge:0,shape:overrides.shape??combat.basic_shape,archetype:d.archetype};
}

// v0.5.19 core-roster whitelist. Legacy units stay in BATTLE_ROSTER for rollback only.
const ACTIVE_PLAYABLE_UNITS=Object.freeze(['Crimson','Sub-Zero','Lebee','Senku']);
const DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM=Object.freeze(['Crimson','Lebee','Sub-Zero']);

function validatePlayableRosterRegistration(){
 const missing=ACTIVE_PLAYABLE_UNITS.filter(name=>!BATTLE_ROSTER[name]);
 if(missing.length){
   console.error('Playable roster registration missing:',missing);
   return false;
 }
 return true;
}
const PLAYABLE_ROSTER_VALID=validatePlayableRosterRegistration();
const TEAM_STORAGE_KEY='blazingBattle.activeTeam.v1';

function validActiveTeam(team){
 return Array.isArray(team)
   && team.length===3
   && new Set(team).size===3
   && team.every(name=>ACTIVE_PLAYABLE_UNITS.includes(name));
}
function getActiveTeam(){
 if(validActiveTeam(window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM))return [...window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM];
 try{
   const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(TEAM_STORAGE_KEY)||'null');
   if(validActiveTeam(saved)){
     window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM=[...saved];
     return [...saved];
   }
 }catch(_){}
 window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM=[...DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM];
 return [...DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM];
}
function setActiveTeam(team){
 if(!validActiveTeam(team))throw new Error('Invalid active team');
 window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM=[...team];
 try{localStorage.setItem(TEAM_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(team));}catch(_){}
 return [...team];
}
function teamSpawnOptions(name){
 // Current development test configuration: Lebee and Sub-Zero remain Jutsu-ready.
 return (name==='Lebee'||name==='Sub-Zero')?{startingChakra:'full'}:{};
}

   && new Set(team).size===3

function getActiveTeam(){
 if(validActiveTeam(window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM))return [...window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM];
 try{
   const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(TEAM_STORAGE_KEY)||'null');
   if(validActiveTeam(saved)){
     window.BLAZING_ACTIVE_TEAM=[...saved];
     return [...saved];
   }
 }catch(_){}

 // Current development test configuration: Lebee and Sub-Zero remain Jutsu-ready.
 return (name==='Lebee'||name==='Sub-Zero')?{startingChakra:'full'}:{};

function buildPlayerPairs(y){
 const xs=[95,240,385];
 const team=getActiveTeam();

 return team.map((name,i)=>{
   if(!BATTLE_ROSTER[name]){
     console.error(`Team unit "${name}" is not registered in BATTLE_ROSTER.`);
     const fallback=DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM.find(n=>BATTLE_ROSTER[n]&&!team.includes(n))
       || DEFAULT_ACTIVE_TEAM.find(n=>BATTLE_ROSTER[n])
       || Object.keys(BATTLE_ROSTER)[0];
     name=fallback;
   }

   return {
     x:xs[i],y,active:0,gauge:0,units:[
       makeRosterUnit(name,teamSpawnOptions(name)),
       emptyReserve()
     ]
   };
 });
}

const ACTIVE_ENEMY_SPRITES=Object.freeze(['Onre','Gotoku','Yurei']);
function emptyReserve(){
 return {name:'—',mark:'',speed:0,attack:0,shape:{type:'circle',r:0},jutsuShape:{type:'circle',r:0},jutsuCost:99,jutsuDamage:0,linkBuff:null,hp:0,maxHp:0,chakra:0,maxChakra:0,rotation:0};
}

 if(img.src!==src){
   img.src=src;
 }
 if(typeof img.decode==='function'){
   img.decode().catch(()=>{});
 }
}

const LEVEL1_PORTRAIT_MAP=new Image();

function setBattleMap(kind){
 const isBoss=kind==='boss';
 const src=isBoss?window.BLAZING_ASSETS.maps.anubisBoss:window.BLAZING_ASSETS.maps.level1;
 const img=isBoss?ANUBIS_PORTRAIT_MAP:LEVEL1_PORTRAIT_MAP;

 // CSS fallback renders immediately even if Edge delays Image() decode.
 cvs.style.backgroundImage=`url("${src}")`;

 if(img.src!==src){
   img.src=src;
 }
 if(typeof img.decode==='function'){
   img.decode().catch(()=>{});
 }
}
setBattleMap('level');

const front=p=>p.units[p.active], back=p=>p.units[1-p.active];
const pairAlive=p=>p.units.some(u=>u.hp>0);
const alivePairs=()=>S.pairs.filter(pairAlive);
const aliveEnemies=()=>S.enemies.filter(e=>e.hp>0);
const NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE={type:'circle',r:43};
const normalShape=()=>NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE;
const attackShape=u=>{
 // Strict separation:
 // NORMAL -> unit's canonical combat.basic_shape
 // JUTSU  -> unit's canonical combat.jutsu_shape
 if(S.action==='jutsu')return u.jutsuShape;

// Ability-tier lookup must live at battle scope because both the ground-field
// pass and the later enemy target-feedback pass use it.
function isUltimateAction(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   // canonicalUnit already resolves both IDs and display names through UNIT_NAME_TO_ID.
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return !!(ability?.ultimate===true || ability?.category==='ultimate' || ability?.tier==='ultimate');
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}

function isAllySupportJutsu(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return ability?.target==='all_allies' || ability?.effect==='heal_full';
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}


function ensureFront(p){
 if(front(p).hp>0)return true;
 if(front(p).hp<=0 && koAnimating(front(p).name))return true;
 if(back(p).hp>0){p.active=1-p.active;return true}
 return false
}
function fxMap(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.unitFX)S.anim.unitFX={};
 return S.anim.unitFX;
}
function unitFX(name){
 return S.anim?.unitFX?.[name]||null;
}
function koAnimating(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration);
}
function renderWhileKO(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration+90);
}
function startHitReaction(name,dir=1,strong=false){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'hit',start:performance.now(),duration:strong?230:180,dir:dir||1,strong:!!strong};
}
function startKOReaction(name,dir=1){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'ko',start:performance.now(),duration:520,dir:dir||1};
}
function pairRenderable(p){
 return pairAlive(p)||renderWhileKO(front(p).name)||renderWhileKO(back(p).name);
}
function handleKO(p,defeatedName,dir=1){
 addFloat(p.x,p.y-58,'KO','#ff7272');
 startKOReaction(defeatedName,dir);
 p.gauge=0;
 if(back(p).hp>0){
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${back(p).name} is preparing to move to the front.`;
   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(front(p).name===defeatedName && back(p).hp>0){
       p.active=1-p.active;
       S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${front(p).name} automatically moved to the front.`;
     }
   },380);
 }else{
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — that pair has been defeated.`;
 }
}
function handleEnemyKO(e,dir=1){
 addFloat(e.x,e.y-46,'KO','#ff7272');
 startKOReaction(e.name,dir);
 e.gauge=0;
 S.log=`${e.name} was defeated.`;
}

function local(p,u,e){let a=-(u.rotation||0),dx=e.x-p.x,dy=e.y-p.y;return{x:dx*Math.cos(a)-dy*Math.sin(a),y:dx*Math.sin(a)+dy*Math.cos(a)}}

function hits(p,u,e){let s=u.shape,q=local(p,u,e),er=e.r||20;
 if(s.type==='screen_rect')return e.x>=BATTLE_BOUNDS.left&&e.x<=BATTLE_BOUNDS.right&&e.y>=BATTLE_BOUNDS.top&&e.y<=BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom;
 if(s.type==='circle')return d(p,e)<=s.r+er;
 if(s.type==='rect'){
   const rq={x:q.x-(s.offset_x||0),y:q.y-(s.offset_y||0)};
   return circleRect(rq,er,s.w/2,s.h/2);
 }

 if(s.type==='cross')return circleRect(q,er,s.l/2,s.t/2)||circleRect(q,er,s.t/2,s.l/2);

 return false}
function drawField(){
  const img=S.bossBattle?ANUBIS_PORTRAIT_MAP:LEVEL1_PORTRAIT_MAP;
  if(img.complete && img.naturalWidth>0){
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
    // Portrait maps are authored for the phone battlefield: crop only if aspect ratios differ slightly.
    const ir=img.naturalWidth/img.naturalHeight, cr=W/H;
    let sx=0,sy=0,sw=img.naturalWidth,sh=img.naturalHeight;
    if(ir>cr){sw=img.naturalHeight*cr;sx=(img.naturalWidth-sw)/2;}
    else if(ir<cr){sh=img.naturalWidth/cr;sy=(img.naturalHeight-sh)/2;}
    // Camera zoom: retain the same battlefield coordinates, but crop the art
    // slightly tighter so combat reads closer on a phone.
    const baseSw=sw,baseSh=sh;
    sw=baseSw/MAP_ZOOM; sh=baseSh/MAP_ZOOM;
    sx+=(baseSw-sw)/2; sy+=(baseSh-sh)/2;
    ctx.drawImage(img,sx,sy,sw,sh,0,0,W,H);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(7,10,12,.025)';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    return;
  }
  // Keep canvas transparent until the stage image is decoded; CSS background is the fallback.
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
}

function updateMeter(){
 [...meter.querySelectorAll('.icon')].forEach(n=>n.remove());
 let entries=[];
 S.pairs.forEach((p,i)=>{if(pairAlive(p)){ensureFront(p);entries.push({label:front(p).mark,name:front(p).name,speed:front(p).speed,g:p.gauge,color:COLORS[i]})}});
 S.enemies.forEach(e=>{if(e.hp>0)entries.push({label:e.boss?'BOSS':'E'+e.mark,name:e.name,speed:e.speed,g:e.gauge,color:'#d84b54'})});
 entries.forEach(e=>{

function tacticalTickerItems(){
 if(!gameStarted || S.phase!=='player' || S.ready?.kind!=='pair')
   return [];

 const attackPreview=S.action==='normal'||S.action==='jutsu'||S.drag;
 if(!attackPreview)return [];

 const activePair=S.ready.ref;
 if(!pairAlive(activePair))return [];

 ensureFront(activePair);

 const members=comboMembers(null,activePair);
 const items=[];

 // Nothing shows until the formation actually enters a valid link area.
 if(members.length>1){
   const normalLinkDamage=members.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+buffedNormalDamage(p).damage,0);
   items.push({
     text:`LINK ATTACK ×${members.length} · ${normalLinkDamage} DMG`,
     tone:'link'
   });
 }

 // Buff currently RECEIVED by the active fighter.
 const incoming=totalLinkAttackBuff(activePair);
 if(incoming.bonus>0){
   items.push({
     text:`LINK ATK +${Math.round(incoming.bonus*100)}%`,
     tone:'buff'
   });
 }

 // Buff currently PROVIDED by the active fighter to linked allies.
 let outgoing=[];
 S.pairs.forEach(other=>{
   if(other===activePair||!pairAlive(other))return;
   const effect=linkBuffFromProvider(activePair,other);
   if(effect.bonus>0)outgoing.push(effect.bonus);
 });
 if(outgoing.length){
   items.push({
     text:`ALLY ATK +${Math.round(Math.max(...outgoing)*100)}%`,
     tone:'buff'
   });
 }

 // The ticker is intentionally generic so Field Skills / Buddy Skills /
 // debuffs can append their own segments later without adding map clutter.
 return items;
}

function updateTacticalTicker(){
 if(!tacticalTicker)return;

 const items=tacticalTickerItems();
 if(!items.length){
   tacticalTicker.classList.remove('show','linkActive');
   tacticalTicker.innerHTML='';
   return;
 }

 tacticalTicker.innerHTML=items
   .map(item=>`<span class="tickerItem ${item.tone||'info'}">${item.text}</span>`)
   .join('');

 tacticalTicker.classList.add('show');
 tacticalTicker.classList.toggle('linkActive',items.some(item=>item.tone==='link'));
}

function addFloat(x,y,text,color){S.floaters.push({x,y,text,color,life:1});let f=S.floaters.at(-1);let id=setInterval(()=>{f.y-=1;f.life-=.025;if(f.life<=0){clearInterval(id);S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==f)}},16)}
function tick(){
 if(!gameStarted||S.phase!=='charge')return;
 // v0.5.10 stability: charge is a non-animation state. A stale render animation
 // must never stop the turn meter. Clear it defensively instead of freezing.
 if(S.anim)S.anim=null;
 let max=null;
 S.pairs.forEach(p=>{if(!pairAlive(p))return;ensureFront(p);p.gauge+=front(p).speed*.018;if(p.gauge>=100&&(!max||p.gauge>max.g))max={kind:'pair',ref:p,g:p.gauge}});
 S.enemies.forEach(e=>{if(e.hp<=0)return;e.gauge+=e.speed*.018;if(e.gauge>=100&&(!max||e.gauge>max.g))max={kind:'enemy',ref:e,g:e.gauge}});
 // v0.5.11 hard rescue: a charge phase may never strand the battlefield.
 if(!S._chargeSince)S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 if(!max && performance.now()-S._chargeSince>3800){
   const rescuePair=alivePairs().sort((a,b)=>b.gauge-a.gauge)[0];
   if(rescuePair){rescuePair.gauge=100;max={kind:'pair',ref:rescuePair,g:100};S.log='Turn system recovered — player control restored.';}
 }
 if(max){
   S.ready=max;
   S._chargeSince=0;
   S.readyStarted=performance.now();
   S.dragOrigin=null;
   S.phase=max.kind==='pair'?'player':'cpu';
   if(max.kind==='enemy')setTimeout(cpuTurn,350)
 }
}
setInterval(tick,30);
function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}
    let push=minDist-len;
    p.x=clamp(p.x+(dx/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right);
    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }
}
function inputPoint(ev){
 let r=cvs.getBoundingClientRect();
 return {x:(ev.clientX-r.left)*W/r.width,y:(ev.clientY-r.top)*H/r.height};
}

// ===== v0.5.15 MOBILE INPUT =====
// Battlefield interaction is touch-first: press the active unit, drag to position,
// and release to commit the selected Normal/Jutsu action.
cvs.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{
 if(S.phase!=='player'||S.anim?.settle)return;
 let p=S.ready?.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev);
 if(!p||!pairAlive(p)||d(pt,p)>UNIT_TOUCH_RADIUS)return;
 ev.preventDefault();
 S.drag=true;
 S.dragOrigin={x:p.x,y:p.y};
 S.dragVisual={scale:1.0,rot:0,lift:0,lastX:pt.x,lastY:pt.y,targetScale:1.34,targetLift:17};
 try{cvs.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId)}catch(_e){}
});

 if(!S.drag)return;
 ev.preventDefault();
 let p=S.ready.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev),v=S.dragVisual;

 let targetRot=clamp(dx*.022,-.34,.34);
 v.rot=clamp(v.rot*.58 + targetRot*.72,-.36,.36);
 let flutter=clamp(Math.abs(dy)*.08,0,2.2);
 v.scale+=(v.targetScale-v.scale)*.32;
 v.lift+=(v.targetLift+flutter-v.lift)*.30;
 v.lastX=pt.x;v.lastY=pt.y;
 let legal=clampToBattlefield(pt);
 p.x=legal.x;p.y=legal.y;
});

 normalBtn.classList.toggle('selected',S.action==='normal');
 jutsuBtn.classList.toggle('selected',S.action==='jutsu');

 let can=S.phase==='player'&&!S.drag&&!S.anim?.settle;
 normalBtn.disabled=!can;swapBtn.disabled=!can||S.ready?.kind!=='pair'||back(S.ready.ref).hp<=0;

 if(S.ready?.kind==='pair'){
   let u=front(S.ready.ref);
   jutsuBtn.textContent=`${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'} · ${u.jutsuCost} ◆`;
   jutsuBtn.disabled=!can||u.chakra<u.jutsuCost
 }else{
   jutsuBtn.textContent='Jutsu';jutsuBtn.disabled=true
 }

 statusEl.innerHTML=S.pairs.map((p,i)=>{
   if(!pairAlive(p))return `<div class="card pair"><b>Pair ${i+1} defeated</b><br><span class="muted">Both fighters KO</span></div>`;
   ensureFront(p);
   let f=front(p),b=back(p);
   if(b.name==='—')return `<div class="card pair ${S.ready?.ref===p?'activePair':''}">
    <b>${f.name}</b><br>
    <span class="muted">${f.hp}/${f.maxHp} HP · ${f.speed} SPD · <span class="chakraText">${f.chakra}/${f.maxChakra} ◆</span></span>
   </div>`;
   return `<div class="card pair ${S.ready?.ref===p?'activePair':''}">
    <b>${f.name}</b> / ${b.name}<br>
    <span class="muted">${f.hp}/${f.maxHp} HP · ${f.speed} SPD · <span class="chakraText">${f.chakra}/${f.maxChakra} ◆</span></span><br>
    <span class="muted">Rear ${b.name}: ${b.hp}/${b.maxHp} HP · ${b.chakra}/${b.maxChakra} ◆</span>
   </div>`
 }).join('')
}

function addFloat(x,y,text,color){S.floaters.push({x,y,text,color,life:1});let f=S.floaters.at(-1);let id=setInterval(()=>{f.y-=1;f.life-=.025;if(f.life<=0){clearInterval(id);S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==f)}},16)}
function tick(){
 if(!gameStarted||S.phase!=='charge')return;
 // v0.5.10 stability: charge is a non-animation state. A stale render animation
 // must never stop the turn meter. Clear it defensively instead of freezing.
 if(S.anim)S.anim=null;
 let max=null;
 S.pairs.forEach(p=>{if(!pairAlive(p))return;ensureFront(p);p.gauge+=front(p).speed*.018;if(p.gauge>=100&&(!max||p.gauge>max.g))max={kind:'pair',ref:p,g:p.gauge}});
 S.enemies.forEach(e=>{if(e.hp<=0)return;e.gauge+=e.speed*.018;if(e.gauge>=100&&(!max||e.gauge>max.g))max={kind:'enemy',ref:e,g:e.gauge}});
 // v0.5.11 hard rescue: a charge phase may never strand the battlefield.
 if(!S._chargeSince)S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 if(!max && performance.now()-S._chargeSince>3800){
   const rescuePair=alivePairs().sort((a,b)=>b.gauge-a.gauge)[0];
   if(rescuePair){rescuePair.gauge=100;max={kind:'pair',ref:rescuePair,g:100};S.log='Turn system recovered — player control restored.';}
 }
 if(max){
   S.ready=max;
   S._chargeSince=0;
   S.readyStarted=performance.now();
   S.dragOrigin=null;
   S.phase=max.kind==='pair'?'player':'cpu';
   if(max.kind==='enemy')setTimeout(cpuTurn,350)
 }
}
setInterval(tick,30);
function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}
    let push=minDist-len;
    p.x=clamp(p.x+(dx/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right);
    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }
}
function inputPoint(ev){
 let r=cvs.getBoundingClientRect();
 return {x:(ev.clientX-r.left)*W/r.width,y:(ev.clientY-r.top)*H/r.height};
}

// ===== v0.5.15 MOBILE INPUT =====
// Battlefield interaction is touch-first: press the active unit, drag to position,
// and release to commit the selected Normal/Jutsu action.
cvs.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{
 if(S.phase!=='player'||S.anim?.settle)return;
 let p=S.ready?.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev);
 if(!p||!pairAlive(p)||d(pt,p)>UNIT_TOUCH_RADIUS)return;
 ev.preventDefault();
 S.drag=true;
 S.dragOrigin={x:p.x,y:p.y};
 S.dragVisual={scale:1.0,rot:0,lift:0,lastX:pt.x,lastY:pt.y,targetScale:1.34,targetLift:17};
 try{cvs.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId)}catch(_e){}
});

cvs.addEventListener('pointermove',ev=>{
 if(!S.drag)return;
 ev.preventDefault();
 let p=S.ready.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev),v=S.dragVisual;
 let dx=pt.x-v.lastX,dy=pt.y-v.lastY;
 let targetRot=clamp(dx*.022,-.34,.34);
 v.rot=clamp(v.rot*.58 + targetRot*.72,-.36,.36);
 let flutter=clamp(Math.abs(dy)*.08,0,2.2);
 v.scale+=(v.targetScale-v.scale)*.32;
 v.lift+=(v.targetLift+flutter-v.lift)*.30;
 v.lastX=pt.x;v.lastY=pt.y;
 let legal=clampToBattlefield(pt);
 p.x=legal.x;p.y=legal.y;
});

 if(!S.drag)return;

     let ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
     let bounce=Math.sin(t*Math.PI

function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}

    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }

function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}
    let push=minDist-len;
    p.x=clamp(p.x+(dx/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right);
    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }
}

 let r=cvs.getBoundingClientRect();
 return {x:(ev.clientX-r.left)*W/r.width,y:(ev.clientY-r.top)*H/r.height};
}

cvs.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{
 if(S.phase!=='player'||S.anim?.settle)return;
 let p=S.ready?.ref,pt=inputPoint(ev);

});

function settleCollision(p){
 const minDist=47;
 const blockers=[...S.pairs.filter(other=>other!==p&&pairAlive(other)),...aliveEnemies()];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  blockers.forEach(b=>{
   let dx=p.x-b.x,dy=p.y-b.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
   if(len<minDist){
    if(len<.01){dx=1;dy=0;len=1}
    let push=minDist-len;
    p.x=clamp(p.x+(dx/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right);
    p.y=clamp(p.y+(dy/len)*push,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom)
   }
  })
 }
}

     if(t<1){requestAnimationFrame(settle);return}

function animateSenkuChemicalReaction(unitName,from,target,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const meta=canonicalUnit('senku').abilities.jutsu.presentation||{};
 const castDuration=meta.cast_duration_ms||680;
 const flightDuration=meta.flight_duration_ms||420;
 const impactDuration=meta.impact_duration_ms||620;

 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'special',start:performance.now(),duration:castDuration};

 if(meta.screen_dim_alpha){
   S.jutsuDim={start:performance.now(),alpha:meta.screen_dim_alpha,end:null};
 }

 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

   const projectile={
     kind:'senkuChemProjectile',
     from:{x:from.x,y:from.y-30},
     to:{x:target.x,y:target.y-10},
     start:performance.now(),
     duration:flightDuration,
     life:1
   };
   S.floaters.push(projectile);

   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     const impact={
       kind:'senkuChemImpact',
       x:target.x,
       y:target.y,
       start:performance.now(),
       duration:impactDuration,
       life:1
     };
     S.floaters.push(impact);

     try{onImpact&&onImpact()}
     catch(err){
       console.error('Senku Chemical Reaction impact failed:',err);
       return recoverAction('Senku Chemical Reaction impact');
     }

     setTimeout(()=>{
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==impact);
       if(S.jutsuDim)S.jutsuDim.end=performance.now();
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

       if(actionTokenAlive(token)){
         try{onDone&&onDone()}
         catch(err){recoverAction('Senku Chemical Reaction completion')}
       }
     },impactDuration);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.70);
}

function animateSenkuBomb(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone,attackKind='punch'){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const meta=canonicalUnit('senku')?.abilities?.basic?.presentation||{};
 const castDuration=meta.cast_duration_ms||560;
 const flightDuration=meta.flight_duration_ms||620;
 const blastDuration=meta.impact_duration_ms||430;
 const arcHeight=meta.arc_height_px||86;

 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 state.attackPose[unitName]={
   kind:attackKind,
   start:performance.now(),
   duration:castDuration
 };

 // Release near the final throwing pose rather than halfway through the cast.
 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

   const projectile={
     kind:'senkuBombProjectile',
     from:{x:from.x,y:from.y-31},
     to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-12},
     start:performance.now(),
     duration:flightDuration,
     arcHeight,
     life:1
   };
   S.floaters.push(projectile);

   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     const targetGroundY=(enemy.feetY??enemy.groundY??enemy.y);
     const blast={
       kind:'senkuExplosion',
       x:enemy.x,
       y:targetGroundY,
       layer:'ground',
       start:performance.now(),
       duration:360,
       life:1
     };
     S.floaters.push(blast);

     // Damage and target recoil happen only when the bomb lands.
     try{
       onImpact&&onImpact();
     }catch(err){
       console.error('Senku bomb impact failed:',err);
       return recoverAction('Senku bomb impact');
     }

     setTimeout(()=>{
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==blast);
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

       if(actionTokenAlive(token)){
         try{
           onDone&&onDone();
         }catch(err){
           recoverAction('Senku bomb completion');
         }
       }
     },blastDuration);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.76);
}

function setMeteorFullscreenFlash(color,opacity=1,transition='none'){
 const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
 if(!el)return;
 el.classList.add('active');
 el.style.transition=transition;
 el.style.background=color;
 el.style.opacity=String(opacity);
}

function clearMeteorFullscreenFlash(){
 const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
 if(!el)return;
 el.style.transition='none';
 el.style.opacity='0';
 el.style.background='transparent';
 el.classList.remove('active');
}

function playLebeeMeteorImpactFlash(done){
 const meta=canonicalUnit('lebee').abilities.jutsu.presentation||{};
 const whiteHold=meta.final_flash_white_hold_ms||170;
 const whiteFade=meta.final_flash_white_fade_ms||620;

 // Final meteor: snap to full white.
 setMeteorFullscreenFlash('#ffffff',1,'none');

 setTimeout(()=>{
   const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
   if(!el){
     done&&done();
     return;
   }
   el.style.transition=`opacity ${whiteFade}ms ease-out`;
   el.style.opacity='0';

   setTimeout(()=>{
     clearMeteorFullscreenFlash();
     done&&done();
   },whiteFade+20);
 },whiteHold);
}

function finishAction(){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 clearActionWatchdog();
 setTimeout(()=>{
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  // v0.5.10: retire the completed action token BEFORE clearing S.anim.
  // This prevents late recoil/requestAnimationFrame callbacks from recreating
  // S.anim after the turn has returned to charge, which caused the hard freeze.
  invalidateActions();
  S.anim=null;S.ready=null;S.action='normal';
  if(S.victoryFX||!aliveEnemies().length){
   if(!S.victoryFX)startVictorySequence();
   S.phase='resolve';
   return;
  }
  if(!alivePairs().length){
   S.phase='resolve';
   S.log='Defeat — all three fighter pairs have been knocked out.';
   return;
  }
  S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 },220)
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.
 for(let pass=0;pass<8;pass++){
   let blocker=null;
   let blockerDist=Infinity;

   S.pairs.forEach(pair=>{
     if(!pairAlive(pair))return;
     let dist=d(enemy,pair);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(other=>{
     if(other===enemy||other.hp<=0)return;
     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(!blocker)break;

   let dx=enemy.x-blocker.x;
   let dy=enemy.y-blocker.y;
   let len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);

   // Exact same-center fallback gets a deterministic escape direction.
   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;
   }

   let push=(minDist-len)+.75;
   enemy.x+=(dx/len)*push;
   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=clampToBattlefield(enemy);
   enemy.

===== animateLebeeStarBlast =====

--- match 1 @ 41334468 ---
pletion callback failed:',err);recoverAction('completion callback')}
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(ret)
  },65)
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(go)
}
function addImpactFlash(x,y,color='#ffffff'){
 let f={x,y,text:'✦',color,life:1,impact:true};
 S.floaters.push(f);
 let id=setInterval(()=>{
   f.y-=.25;
   f.life-=.07;
   if(f.life<=0){
     clearInterval(id);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(item=>item!==f);
   }
 },16);
}
function animateFreezeBlast(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const castDuration=340;
 const flightDuration=420;
 const freezeHold=520;
 const totalDuration=castDuration+flightDuration+freezeHold;
 const castStart=performance.now();
 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 // The four user-supplied cast frames are played by drawUnit via
 // ATTACK_SPRITES['Sub-Zero'].freeze while he remains planted in place.
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'freeze',start:castStart,duration:castDuration+flightDuration};

 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   const projectileStart=performance.now();
   const projectile={kind:'iceProjectile',from:{x:from.x+10,y:from.y-22},to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-18},start:projectileStart,duration:flightDuration,life:1};
   S.floaters.push(projectile);
   function fly(now){
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
     const t=Math.min(1,(now-projectileStart)/flightDuration);
     if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(fly);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Freeze Blast impact failed:',err);return recoverAction('freeze blast impact')}
     const ice={kind:'frozenTarget',x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,start:performance.now(),life:1};
     S.floaters.push(ice);
     setTimeout(()=>{
       if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==ice);
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];
       try{onDone&&onDone()}catch(err){console.error('Freeze Blast completion failed:',err);recoverAction('freeze blast completion')}
     },freezeHold);
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(fly);
 },castDuration);
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.
 for(let pass=0;pass<8;pass++){
   let blocker=null;
   let blockerDist=Infinity;

   S.pairs.forEach(pair=>{
     if(!pairAlive(pair))return;
     let dist=d(enemy,pair);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(other=>{
     if(other===enemy||other.hp<=0)return;
     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(!blocker)break;

   let dx=enemy.x-blocker.x;
   let dy=enemy.y-blocker.y;
   let len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);

   // Exact same-center fallback gets a deterministic escape direction.
   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;
   }

   let push=(minDist-len)+.75;
   enemy.x+=(dx/len)*push;
   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=

}
function armActionWatchdog(reason='combat action',token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN){
 clearActionWatchdog();
 ACTION_WATCHDOG=setTimeout(()=>{
   if(actionTokenAlive(token)&&gameStarted&&(S.phase==='resolve'||S.phase==='cpu'))recoverAction(reason);
 },2600);
}
function ensureAnimState(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.positions)S.anim.positions={};
 return S.anim;
}
function animateLunge(unitName,from,to,onImpact,onDone,attackKind='punch'){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 let start=performance.now(),dur=175,backDur=145;
 let anim=ensureAnimState();
 if(!anim.attackPose)anim.attackPose={};
 anim.attackPose[unitName]={kind:attackKind,start,duration:dur+150};
 function go(now){
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  let state=ensureAnimState();
  let t=Math.min(1,(now-start)/dur),ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
  state.positions[unitName]={x:from.x+(to.x-from.x)*ease,y:from.y+(to.y-from.y)*ease};
  if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(go);
  try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Attack impact callback failed:',err);return recoverAction('impact callback')}
  setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   let bs=performance.now();
   function ret(n){
    if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
    let st=ensureAnimState();
    let q=Math.min(1,(n-bs)/backDur),e=1-Math.pow(1-q,3);
    st.positions[unitName]={x:to.x+(from.x-to.x)*e,y:to.y+(from.y-to.y)*e};
    if(q<1)return requestAnimationFrame(ret);
    delete st.positions[unitName];
    if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];
    try{onDone&&onDone()}catch(err){console.error('Attack completion callback failed:',err);recoverAction('completion callback')}
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(ret)
  },65)
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(go)
}
function addImpactFlash(x,y,color='#ffffff'){
 let f={x,y,text:'✦',color,life:1,impact:true};
 S.floaters.push(f);
 let id=setInterval(()=>{
   f.y-=.25;
   f.life-=.07;
   if(f.life<=0){
     clearInterval(id);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(item=>item!==f);
   }
 },16);
}
function animateFreezeBlast(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const castDuration=340;
 const flightDuration=420;
 const freezeHold=520;
 const totalDuration=castDuration+flightDuration+freezeHold;
 const castStart=performance.now();
 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 // The four user-supplied cast frames are played by drawUnit via
 // ATTACK_SPRITES['Sub-Zero'].freeze while he remains planted in place.
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'freeze',start:castStart,duration:castDuration+flightDuration};

 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   const projectileStart=performance.now();
   const projectile={kind:'iceProjectile',from:{x:from.x+10,y:from.y-22},to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-18},start:projectileStart,duration:flightDuration,life:1};
   S.floaters.push(projectile);
   function fly(now){
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
     const t=Math.min(1,(now-projectileStart)/flightDuration);
     if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(fly);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Freeze Blast impact failed:',err);return recoverAction('freeze blast impact')}
     const ice={kind:'frozenTarget',x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,start:performance.now(),life:1};
     S.floaters.push(ice);
     setTimeout(()=>{
       if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==ice);
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];
       try{onDone&&onDone()}catch(err){console.error('Freeze Blast completion failed:',err);recoverAction('freeze blast completion')}
     },freezeHold);
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(fly);
 },castDuration);
}


function animateLebeeStarBlast(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const castDuration=320,flightDuration=300;
 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'punch',start:performance.now(),duration:castDuration+flightDuration};
 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   const projectile={kind:'lebeeStarProjectile',from:{x:from.x,y:from.y-20},to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-12},start:performance.now(),duration:flightDuration,life:1};
   S.floaters.push(projectile);
   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Lebee star blast impact failed:',err);return recoverAction('Lebee basic impact')}
     const st=ensureAnimState();if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];
     setTimeout(()=>{if(actionTokenAlive(token)){try{onDone&&onDone()}catch(err){recoverAction('Lebee basic completion')}}},90);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.48);
}

let S=fresh();

const front=p=>p.units[p.active], back=p=>p.units[1-p.active];
const pairAlive=p=>p.units.some(u=>u.hp>0);
const alivePairs=()=>S.pairs.filter(pairAlive);
const aliveEnemies=()=>S.enemies.filter(e=>e.hp>0);
const NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE={type:'circle',r:43};
const normalShape=()=>NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE;
const attackShape=u=>{
 // Strict separation:
 // NORMAL -> unit's canonical combat.basic_shape
 // JUTSU  -> unit's canonical combat.jutsu_shape
 if(S.action==='jutsu')return u.jutsuShape;
 return u.shape||normalShape();
};
function attackProxy(u){return {...u,shape:attackShape(u)}}

// Ability-tier lookup must live at battle scope because both the ground-field
// pass and the later enemy target-feedback pass use it.
function isUltimateAction(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   // canonicalUnit already resolves both IDs and display names through UNIT_NAME_TO_ID.
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return !!(ability?.ultimate===true || ability?.category==='ultimate' || ability?.tier==='ultimate');
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}

function isAllySupportJutsu(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return ability?.target==='all_allies' || ability?.effect==='heal_full';
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}


function ensureFront(p){
 if(front(p).hp>0)return true;
 if(front(p).hp<=0 && koAnimating(front(p).name))return true;
 if(back(p).hp>0){p.active=1-p.active;return true}
 return false
}
function fxMap(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.unitFX)S.anim.unitFX={};
 return S.anim.unitFX;
}
function unitFX(name){
 return S.anim?.unitFX?.[name]||null;
}
function koAnimating(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration);
}
function renderWhileKO(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration+90);
}
function startHitReaction(name,dir=1,strong=false){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'hit',start:performance.now(),duration:strong?230:180,dir:dir||1,strong:!!strong};
}
function startKOReaction(name,dir=1){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'ko',start:performance.now(),duration:520,dir:dir||1};
}
function pairRenderable(p){
 return pairAlive(p)||renderWhileKO(front(p).name)||renderWhileKO(back(p).name);
}
function handleKO(p,defeatedName,dir=1){
 addFloat(p.x,p.y-58,'KO','#ff7272');
 startKOReaction(defeatedName,dir);
 p.gauge=0;
 if(back(p).hp>0){
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${back(p).name} is preparing to move to the front.`;
   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(front(p).name===defeatedName && back(p).hp>0){
       p.active=1-p.active;
       S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${front(p).name} automatically moved to the front.`;
     }
   },380);
 }else{
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — that pair has been defeated.`;
 }
}
function handleEnemyKO(e,dir=1){
 addFloat(e.x,e.y-46,'KO','#ff7272');
 startKOReaction(e.name,dir);
 e.gauge=0;
 S.log=`${e.name} was defeated.`;
}

function local(p,u,e){let a=-(u.rotation||0),dx=e.x-p.x,dy=e.y-p.y;return{x:dx*Math.cos(a)-dy*Math.sin(a),y:dx*Math.sin(a)+dy*Math.cos(a)}}
function circleRect(q,r,hw,hh){let x=clamp(q.x,-hw,hw),y=clamp(q.y,-hh,hh);return Math.hypot(q.x-x,q.y-y)<=r}
function hits(p,u,e){let s=u.shape,q=local(p,u,e),er=e.r||20;
 if(s.type==='screen_rect')return e.x>=BATTLE_BOUNDS.left&&e.x<=BATTLE_BOUNDS.right&&e.y>=BATTLE_BOUNDS.top&&e.y<=BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom;
 if(s.type==='circle')return d(p,e)<=s.r+er;
 if(s.type==='rect'){
   const rq={x:q.x-(s.offset_x||0),y:q.y-(s.offset_y||0)};
   return circleRect(rq,er,s.w/2,s.h/2);
 }
 if(s.type==='square')return circleRect(q,er,s.s/2,s.s/2);
 if(s.type==='cross')return circleRect(q,er,s.l/2,s.t/2)||circleRect(q,er,s.t/2,s.l/2);
 if(s.type==='donut'){let z=Math.hypot(q.x,q.y);return z+er>=s.i&&z-er<=s.o}
 if(s.type==='cone'){let z=Math.hypot(q.x,q.y),ang=Math.abs(Math.atan2(q.y,q.x)),pad=Math.asin(Math.min(1,er/Math.max(z,.01)));return q.x+er>=0&&z-er<=s.r&&ang<=s.a/2+pad}
 return false}

function tacticalTickerItems(){
 if(!gameStarted || S.phase!=='player' || S.ready?.kind!=='pair')
   return [];

 const attackPreview=S.action==='normal'||S.action==='jutsu'||S.drag;
 if(!attackPreview)return [];

 const activePair=S.ready.ref;
 if(!pairAlive(activePair))return [];

 ensureFront(activePair);

 const members=comboMembers(null,activePair);
 const items=[];

 // Nothing shows until the formation actually enters a valid link area.
 if(members.length>1){
   const normalLinkDamage=members.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+buffedNormalDamage(p).damage,0);
   items.push({
     text:`LINK ATTACK ×${members.length} · ${normalLinkDamage} DMG`,
     tone:'link'
   });
 }

 // Buff currently RECEIVED by the active fighter.
 const incoming=totalLinkAttackBuff(activePair);
 if(incoming.bonus>0){
   items.push({
     text:`LINK ATK +${Math.round(incoming.bonus*100)}%`,
     tone:'buff'
   });
 }

 // Buff currently PROVIDED by the active fighter to linked allies.
 let outgoing=[];
 S.pairs.forEach(other=>{
   if(other===activePair||!pairAlive(other))return;
   const effect=linkBuffFromProvider(activePair,other);
   if(effect.bonus>0)outgoing.push(effect.bonus);
 });
 if(outgoing.length){
   items.push({
     text:`ALLY ATK +${Math.round(Math.max(...outgoing)*100)}%`,
     tone:'buff'
   });
 }

 // The ticker is intentionally generic so Field Skills / Buddy Skills /
 // debuffs can append their own segments later without adding map clutter.
 return items;
}

function updateTacticalTicker(){
 if(!tacticalTicker)return;

 const items=tacticalTickerItems();
 if(!items.length){
   tacticalTicker.classList.remove('show','linkActive');
   tacticalTicker.innerHTML='';
   return;
 }

 tacticalTicker.innerHTML=items
   .map(item=>`<span class="tickerItem ${item.tone||'info'}">${item.text}</span>`)
   .join('');

 tacticalTicker.classList.add('show');
 tacticalTicker.classList.toggle('linkActive',items.some(item=>item.tone==='link'));
}

 }else{

    <span class="muted">Rear ${b.name}: ${b.hp}/${b.maxHp} HP · ${b.chakra}/${b.maxChakra} ◆</span>
   </div>`
 }).join('')
}

function recoil(enemy,done,dir=1,strong=false){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 startHitReaction(enemy.name,dir,strong);
 let ox=enemy.x,oy=enemy.y,dx=18*(dir||1),start=performance.now();
 function f(n){
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  let state=ensureAnimState(),t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/130),off=Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*dx;
  state.positions[enemy.name]={x:ox+off,y:oy};
  if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(f);
  delete state.positions[enemy.name];
  try{done&&done()}catch(err){console.error('Recoil callback failed:',err);recoverAction('recoil callback')}
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(f)
}
function resolvePlayer(){

 let p=S.ready.ref,u=front(p),useJutsu=S.action==='jutsu',proxy=attackProxy(u);
 const supportJutsu=useJutsu&&isAllySupportJutsu(u);
 let targets=supportJutsu?[]:aliveEnemies().filter(e=>hits(p,proxy,e));
 p.gauge=0;S.phase='resolve';S.anim={positions:{}};const playerActionToken=beginActionToken();armActionWatchdog('player attack',playerActionToken);

 if(useJutsu&&u.chakra<u.jutsuCost){
  S.log=`${u.name} does not have enough chakra.`;S.action='normal';return finishAction()
 }

 if(supportJutsu){
  u.chakra-=u.jutsuCost;

  const allies=alivePairs().filter(pair=>front(pair).hp>0);
  if(!allies.length){
   S.log=`${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'}, but no allies can be healed.`;
   return finishAction();
  }

  const teamCenter={
   x:allies.reduce((sum,pair)=>sum+pair.x,0)/allies.length,
   y:allies.reduce((sum,pair)=>sum+pair.y,0)/allies.length-18
  };

  const from={x:p.x,y:p.y};
  S.log=`${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Revival Formula'} — restoring all active allies to full HP.`;

  return animateSenkuChemicalReaction(
   u.name,from,teamCenter,
   ()=>{
    allies.forEach(pair=>{
     const ally=front(pair);
     const healed=Math.max(0,ally.maxHp-ally.hp);
     ally.hp=ally.maxHp;

     addImpactFlash(pair.x,pair.y-18,'#64ff7d');
     if(healed>0){
      addFloat(pair.x,pair.y-40,`+${healed}`,'#74ff91');
     }else{
      addFloat(pair.x,pair.y-40,'FULL','#74ff91');
     }
    });
   },
   ()=>finishAction()
  );
 }

 if(!targets.length){
  S.log=`${u.name} committed the move but caught no target.`;return finishAction()
 }

 if(useJutsu){
  u.chakra-=u.jutsuCost;

  if(u.name==='Lebee'){
   S.log=`Lebee activated Meteor Jutsu — ${targets.length} target${targets.length===1?'':'s'} caught in the cosmic impact zone.`;
   const from={x:p.x,y:p.y};
   return animateLebeeMeteorJutsu(
    u.name,from,targets,
    ()=>{
     targets.forEach(enemy=>{
      if(enemy.hp<=0)return;
      enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-u.jutsuDamage);
      addImpactFlash(enemy.x,enemy.y-8,'#ff7ae6');
      addFloat(enemy.x,enemy.y-34,'-'+u.jutsuDamage,'#ff8be8');
      const dir=Math.sign(enemy.x-p.x)||1;
      if(enemy.hp<=0)handleEnemyKO(enemy,dir); else recoil(enemy,()=>{},dir,true);
     });
     checkVictoryKillshot();
    },
    ()=>finishAction()
   );
  }

  // Every Jutsu target gets:
  //   active fighter's Jutsu hit
  //   + normal attacks from OTHER allies whose normal range covers that target.
  let jutsuQueue=targets.map(enemy=>{
    let helpers=comboMembers(enemy,p).filter(helper=>helper!==p);
    return {enemy,helpers};
  });

  let anyHelpers=jutsuQueue.some(item=>item.helpers.length>0);

    ? `${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'} — normal combo partners are joining where their ranges overlap.`

function recoil(enemy,done,dir=1,strong=false){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 startHitReaction(enemy.name,dir,strong);
 let ox=enemy.x,oy=enemy.y,dx=18*(dir||1),start=performance.now();
 function f(n){
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  let state=ensureAnimState(),t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/130),off=Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*dx;
  state.positions[enemy.name]={x:ox+off,y:oy};
  if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(f);
  delete state.positions[enemy.name];
  try{done&&done()}catch(err){console.error('Recoil callback failed:',err);recoverAction('recoil callback')}
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(f)
}
function resolvePlayer(){

 let p=S.ready.ref,u=front(p),useJutsu=S.action==='jutsu',proxy=attackProxy(u);
 const supportJutsu=useJutsu&&isAllySupportJutsu(u);
 let targets=supportJutsu?[]:aliveEnemies().filter(e=>hits(p,proxy,e));
 p.gauge=0;S.phase='resolve';S.anim={positions:{}};const playerActionToken=beginActionToken();armActionWatchdog('player attack',playerActionToken);

 if(useJutsu&&u.chakra<u.jutsuCost){
  S.log=`${u.name} does not have enough chakra.`;S.action='normal';return finishAction()
 }

 if(supportJutsu){
  u.chakra-=u.jutsuCost;

  const allies=alivePairs().filter(pair=>front(pair).hp>0);
  if(!allies.length){
   S.log=`${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'}, but no allies can be healed.`;
   return finishAction();
  }

  const teamCenter={
   x:allies.reduce((sum,pair)=>sum+pair.x,0)/allies.length,
   y:allies.reduce((sum,pair)=>sum+pair.y,0)/allies.length-18
  };

  const from={x:p.x,y:p.y};
  S.log=`${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Revival Formula'} — restoring all active allies to full HP.`;

  return animateSenkuChemicalReaction(
   u.name,from,teamCenter,
   ()=>{
    allies.forEach(pair=>{
     const ally=front(pair);
     const healed=Math.max(0,ally.maxHp-ally.hp);
     ally.hp=ally.maxHp;

     addImpactFlash(pair.x,pair.y-18,'#64ff7d');
     if(healed>0){
      addFloat(pair.x,pair.y-40,`+${healed}`,'#74ff91');
     }else{
      addFloat(pair.x,pair.y-40,'FULL','#74ff91');
     }
    });
   },
   ()=>finishAction()
  );
 }

 if(!targets.length){
  S.log=`${u.name} committed the move but caught no target.`;return finishAction()
 }

 if(useJutsu){
  u.chakra-=u.jutsuCost;

  if(u.name==='Lebee'){
   S.log=`Lebee activated Meteor Jutsu — ${targets.length} target${targets.length===1?'':'s'} caught in the cosmic impact zone.`;
   const from={x:p.x,y:p.y};
   return animateLebeeMeteorJutsu(
    u.name,from,targets,
    ()=>{
     targets.forEach(enemy=>{
      if(enemy.hp<=0)return;
      enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-u.jutsuDamage);
      addImpactFlash(enemy.x,enemy.y-8,'#ff7ae6');
      addFloat(enemy.x,enemy.y-34,'-'+u.jutsuDamage,'#ff8be8');
      const dir=Math.sign(enemy.x-p.x)||1;
      if(enemy.hp<=0)handleEnemyKO(enemy,dir); else recoil(enemy,()=>{},dir,true);
     });
     checkVictoryKillshot();
    },
    ()=>finishAction()
   );
  }

  // Every Jutsu target gets:
  //   active fighter's Jutsu hit
  //   + normal attacks from OTHER allies whose normal range covers that target.
  let jutsuQueue=targets.map(enemy=>{
    let helpers=comboMembers(enemy,p).filter(helper=>helper!==p);
    return {enemy,helpers};
  });

  let anyHelpers=jutsuQueue.some(item=>item.helpers.length>0);
  S.log=anyHelpers
    ? `${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'} — normal combo partners are joining where their ranges overlap.`
    : `${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'} — ${targets.length} target${targets.length===1?'':'s'} caught in the field.`;

  let targetIndex=0;

  function runJutsuTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=jutsuQueue.length)return finishAction();

   let item=jutsuQueue[targetIndex++];
   let enemy=item.enemy;
   let helpers=item.helpers;

   // First: the active fighter performs the Jutsu hit.
   let dx=enemy.x-p.x,dy=enemy.y-p.y,len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
   let to={x:enemy.x-dx/len*((enemy.r||19)+27),y:enemy.y-dy/len*((enemy.r||19)+27)};
   let from={x:p.x,y:p.y};

   const runActiveJutsu=(u.name==='Sub-Zero')?animateFreezeBlast:animateLunge;
   const activeFrom=(u.name==='Sub-Zero')?from:from;
   const activeTarget=(u.name==='Sub-Zero')?enemy:to;
   runActiveJutsu(
    u.name,activeFrom,activeTarget,
    ()=>{
     enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-u.jutsuDamage);
     addImpactFlash(enemy.x,enemy.y-8,u.name==='Crimson'?'#ff405c':'#8cecff');
     addFloat(enemy.x,enemy.y-34,'-'+u.jutsuDamage,'#57e7ff');
     let jDir=Math.sign(enemy.x-p.x)||1;
     if(enemy.hp<=0)handleEnemyKO(enemy,jDir); else if(u.name!=='Sub-Zero')recoil(enemy,()=>{},jDir,true);
     if(u.name==='Sub-Zero')enemy.gauge=Math.max(0,(enemy.gauge||0)-35);
     checkVictoryKillshot();
    },
    ()=>{
     let helperIndex=0;

     function runHelper(){
      if(enemy.hp<=0 || helperIndex>=helpers.length)
       return setTimeout(runJutsuTarget,110);

      let hp=helpers[helperIndex++];
      let hu=front(hp);
      let hdx=enemy.x-hp.x,hdy=enemy.y-hp.y,hlen=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(hdx,hdy));

===== target_mode =====

===== onImpact =====

--- match 1 @ 41331093 ---
 if(providerPair===receiverPair)return;
   let result=linkBuffFromProvider(providerPair,receiverPair);
   if(result.bonus>0){
     total+=result.bonus;
     bestRatio=Math.max(bestRatio,result.ratio);
     providers.push(result.provider.name);
   }
 });
 return {bonus:total,ratio:bestRatio,providers};
}

function buffedNormalDamage(pair){
 let u=front(pair);
 let buff=totalLinkAttackBuff(pair);
 return {
   base:u.attack,
   buff,
   damage:Math.round(u.attack*(1+buff.bonus))
 };
}

function comboMembers(enemy,initiatorPair){
 // Enemy argument is retained because the attack sequence calls this per target,
 // but combo membership itself is determined only by ally-to-ally range linking.
 if(!pairAlive(initiatorPair))return [];
 ensureFront(initiatorPair);

 return S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   return pair===initiatorPair || unitsMutuallyLinked(initiatorPair,pair)
 })
}
let ACTION_WATCHDOG=null;
let ACTION_SEQUENCE=0;
let ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN=0;
function clearActionWatchdog(){
 if(ACTION_WATCHDOG){clearTimeout(ACTION_WATCHDOG);ACTION_WATCHDOG=null}
}
function beginActionToken(){
 clearActionWatchdog();
 ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN=++ACTION_SEQUENCE;
 return ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
}
function invalidateActions(){
 clearActionWatchdog();
 ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN=++ACTION_SEQUENCE;
}
function actionTokenAlive(token){return token===ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN}
function recoverAction(reason='animation timeout'){
 console.warn('Blazing Battle recovered action:',reason);
 invalidateActions();
 S.anim=null;S.drag=false;S.dragVisual=null;S.dragOrigin=null;S.action='normal';S.ready=null;
 if(aliveEnemies().length&&alivePairs().length){S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();S.log='Action recovered — turn meter resumed.'}
}
function armActionWatchdog(reason='combat action',token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN){
 clearActionWatchdog();
 ACTION_WATCHDOG=setTimeout(()=>{
   if(actionTokenAlive(token)&&gameStarted&&(S.phase==='resolve'||S.phase==='cpu'))recoverAction(reason);
 },2600);
}
function ensureAnimState(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.positions)S.anim.positions={};
 return S.anim;
}
function animateLunge(unitName,from,to,onImpact,onDone,attackKind='punch'){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 let start=performance.now(),dur=175,backDur=145;
 let anim=ensureAnimState();
 if(!anim.attackPose)anim.attackPose={};
 anim.attackPose[unitName]={kind:attackKind,start,duration:dur+150};
 function go(now){
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  let state=ensureAnimState();
  let t=Math.min(1,(now-start)/dur),ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
  state.positions[unitName]={x:from.x+(to.x-from.x)*ease,y:from.y+(to.y-from.y)*ease};
  if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(go);
  try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Attack impact callback failed:',err);return recoverAction('impact callback')}
  setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   let bs=performance.now();
   function ret(n){
    if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
    let st=ensureAnimState();
    let q=Math.min(1,(n-bs)/backDur),e=1-Math.pow(1-q,3);
    st.positions[unitName]={x:to.x+(from.x-to.x)*e,y:to.y+(from.y-to.y)*e};
    if(q<1)return requestAnimationFrame(ret);
    delete st.positions[unitName];
    if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];
    try{onDone&&onDone()}catch(err){console.error('Attack completion callback failed:',err);recoverAction('completion callback')}
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(ret)
  },65)
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(go)
}
function addImpactFlash(x,y,color='#ffffff'){
 let f={x,y,text:'✦',color,life:1,impact:true};
 S.floaters.push(f);
 let id=setInterval(()=>{
   f.y-=.25;
   f.life-=.07;
   if(f.life<=0){
     clearInterval(id);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(item=>item!==f);
   }
 },16);
}

function armActionWatchdog(reason='combat action',token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN){
 clearActionWatchdog();
 ACTION_WATCHDOG=setTimeout(()=>{
   if(actionTokenAlive(token)&&gameStarted&&(S.phase==='resolve'||S.phase==='cpu'))recoverAction(reason);
 },2600);
}
function ensureAnimState(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.positions)S.anim.positions={};
 return S.anim;
}
function animateLunge(unitName,from,to,onImpact,onDone,attackKind='punch'){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 let start=performance.now(),dur=175,backDur=145;
 let anim=ensureAnimState();
 if(!anim.attackPose)anim.attackPose={};
 anim.attackPose[unitName]={kind:attackKind,start,duration:dur+150};
 function go(now){
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  let state=ensureAnimState();
  let t=Math.min(1,(now-start)/dur),ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
  state.positions[unitName]={x:from.x+(to.x-from.x)*ease,y:from.y+(to.y-from.y)*ease};
  if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(go);
  try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Attack impact callback failed:',err);return recoverAction('impact callback')}
  setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   let bs=performance.now();
   function ret(n){
    if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
    let st=ensureAnimState();
    let q=Math.min(1,(n-bs)/backDur),e=1-Math.pow(1-q,3);
    st.positions[unitName]={x:to.x+(from.x-to.x)*e,y:to.y+(from.y-to.y)*e};
    if(q<1)return requestAnimationFrame(ret);
    delete st.positions[unitName];
    if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];
    try{onDone&&onDone()}catch(err){console.error('Attack completion callback failed:',err);recoverAction('completion callback')}
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(ret)
  },65)
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(go)
}
function addImpactFlash(x,y,color='#ffffff'){
 let f={x,y,text:'✦',color,life:1,impact:true};
 S.floaters.push(f);
 let id=setInterval(()=>{
   f.y-=.25;
   f.life-=.07;
   if(f.life<=0){
     clearInterval(id);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(item=>item!==f);
   }
 },16);
}
function animateFreezeBlast(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const castDuration=340;
 const flightDuration=420;
 const freezeHold=520;
 const totalDuration=castDuration+flightDuration+freezeHold;
 const castStart=performance.now();
 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 // The four user-supplied cast frames are played by drawUnit via
 // ATTACK_SPRITES['Sub-Zero'].freeze while he remains planted in place.
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'freeze',start:castStart,duration:castDuration+flightDuration};

   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
   const projectileStart=performance.now();
   const projectile={kind:'iceProjectile',from:{x:from.x+10,y:from.y-22},to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-18},start:projectileStart,duration:flightDuration,life:1};
   S.floaters.push(projectile);

     const t=Math.min(1,(now-projectileStart)/flightDuration);
     if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(fly);
     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     try{onImpact&&onImpact()}catch(err){console.error('Freeze Blast impact failed:',err);return recoverAction('freeze blast impact')}
     const ice={kind:'frozenTarget',x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,start:performance.now(),life:1};
     S.floaters.push(ice);
     setTimeout(()=>{
